\displaystyle|e^{\theta Y}\mathcal{M}_{4}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Estimates about
\mathcal{E}^{(0)}_{j}(Y)
for
j=1,2,3
.
By Lemma
3.4
and (
3.25
), we obtain that for any
Y\geq 0
,

(3.31)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|e^{\theta Y}\mathcal{E}^{(0)}_{1}(Y)|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}},\\
|e^{\theta Y}\mathcal{E}^{(0)}_{2}(Y)|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}),\\
|e^{\theta Y}\mathcal{E}^{(0)}_{3}(Y)|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

Therefore, by (
3.16
), (
3.20
), (
3.29
), (
3.30
) and (
3.31
), we deduce that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|e^{\theta Y}\psi_{app,s}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\log\hat{c}_{i}|(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+(Y-Y_{c})\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{N}^{-}})\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log\hat{c}_{i}|\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Estimates about
\partial_{Y}\psi_{app,s}(Y)
.
By a direct calculation, we write

(3.32)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{Y}\psi_{app,s}(Y)=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\mathcal{M}_{j}^{(1)}(Y)+\mathcal{E}^{(1)}_{1}(Y)+\mathcal{E}^{(1)}_{2}(Y),\end{split}

where

(3.33)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{M}_{1}^{(1)}(Y):=&-A_{1}(1,Y)A_{2}(0)\partial_{Y}\eta(0)^{-1}\partial_{Y}G(0)+A_{1}(1,Y)\partial_{Y}A_{2}(0)\partial_{Y}\eta(0)^{-1}G(0),\\
\mathcal{M}_{2}^{(1)}(Y):=&A_{1}(1,Y)\int_{0}^{Y}\partial_{Z}^{2}A_{2}(\partial_{Z}\eta)^{-1}G(Z)dZ+A_{2}(1,Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Z}^{2}A_{1}(\partial_{Z}\eta)^{-1}G(Z)dZ,\\
\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}(Y):=&-\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}G(Z)dZ,\\
\mathcal{E}_{1}^{(1)}(Y):=&A_{1}(1,Y)\int_{0}^{Y}\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{2}}(A_{2}(Z)\partial_{Z}G(Z)-\partial_{Z}A_{2}(Z)G(Z))dZ\\
&+A_{2}(1,Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{2}}(A_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}G(Z)-\partial_{Z}A_{1}(Z)G(Z))dZ,\\
\mathcal{E}_{2}^{(1)}(Y):=&\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{2}}\mathcal{H}_{2}(Z)dZ.\end{split}

Estimates about
\mathcal{M}^{(1)}_{1}(Y)
.
According to Lemma
3.1
, we have for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|(u(Y)-\hat{c})|\lesssim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta(Y)|.

Hence, by a similar argument as in the estimates (
3.15
) and (
3.16
),

(3.34)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{1}^{(1)}(Y)|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}e^{-\theta Y}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).\end{split}

Estimates about
\mathcal{M}_{2}^{(1)}(Y)
.
By Lemma
3.4
, we notice that for any
Y
such that
|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1
,

(3.35)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{2}^{(1)}(Y)|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-1}\int_{\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}\frac{|u(Y)-\hat{c}||\eta(Z)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)|}{|\eta(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)|}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ\\
&+C|\varepsilon|^{-1}\int_{\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}\frac{|u(Y)-\hat{c}||\eta(Z)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)|}{|\eta(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)|}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ\\
&+C|\varepsilon|^{-1}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|u(Y)-\hat{c}||\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)|^{-1}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ.\end{split}

By Lemma
3.1
, we can obtain that
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1\},

\displaystyle\frac{|\eta(Z)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)|}{|\eta(Y)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)|}\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} &1,\quad Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y),\\
&|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{3}{4}},\quad Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y),\end{aligned}\right.

and
|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)|^{-1}\lesssim 1
. Hence, by a similar argument as in the estimates about
\mathcal{M}_{2}(Y)
, we can infer that for any
|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1
,

(3.36)

\displaystyle|(u(Y)-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{2}^{(1)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

For any
|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1
, notice that

\displaystyle|(u(Y)-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{2}^{(1)}(Y)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\int_{\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)|e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\int_{\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)|e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\int_{\mathcal{N}}|G(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{0}^{\tilde{Y}}|u(Z)-\hat{c}|^{-1}dZ\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}},

which along with (
3.36
) implies that for any
Y\geq 0
,

(3.37)

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{2}^{(1)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Estimates about
\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}(Y)
.

By the definition of
\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}(Y)
, we have that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}(Y)=

\displaystyle-\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Z}\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}G(Z)dZ

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(Y)\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)^{-1}G(Y)+\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(Z)\partial_{Z}(\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}G(Z))dZ,

which implies that

(3.38)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}(Y)|\\
&\leq|\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(Y)\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)^{-1}(u-\hat{c})G(Y)|+\int_{Y}^{+\infty}|u(Y)-\hat{c}||\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(Z)||\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}\partial_{Z}G(Z)|dZ\\
&\quad+\int_{Y}^{+\infty}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(Z)|\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{2}}G(Z)|dZ.\end{split}

By Lemma
3.4
, we can obtain that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(Y)\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)^{-1}(u-\hat{c})G(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Moreover, by a similar arguments as in the estimates about
\mathcal{M}_{3}
, we can deduce that for
Y\in\mathcal{N}\cup\mathcal{N}^{+}
,

(3.39)

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}|e^{-\theta Y}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

For any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
, we notice that

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}|u(Y)-\hat{c}||\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)|G(Z)|dZ,

which along with the fact that

\displaystyle\int_{Y}^{Y_{-}}|u(Y)-\hat{c}||\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)|G(Z)|dZ\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}|\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Y}^{Y^{-}}|u(Z)-\hat{c}|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}},

implies that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}||\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Therefore, we obtain that for any
Y\geq 0
,

(3.40)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|(u-\hat{c})e^{-\theta Y}\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}(Y)|\\
&\lesssim|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}||\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

By (
3.25
) and similar calculations as above, we can obtain that for any
Y\geq 0

(3.41)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{j=1}^{2}|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{E}^{(1)}_{j}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).\end{split}

Therefore, by (
3.34
), (
3.37
), (
3.40
) and (
3.41
), we conclude that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})e^{-\theta Y}\partial_{Y}\psi_{app,s}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}||\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Estimates about
\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{app,s}(Y)
.
By integration by parts, we have that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,Y\geq Y_{c}\}
,

(3.42)

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{app,s}(Y)=

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1,1}^{(2)}(Y)+\mathcal{M}_{2,1}^{(2)}(Y)+\mathcal{E}_{1}^{(2)}(Y),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1,1}^{(2)}(Y)=

\displaystyle-A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(0)\partial_{Y}\eta(0)^{-1}\partial_{Y}G(0)+A_{1}(Y)\partial_{Y}A_{2}(Y_{-})\partial_{Y}\eta(Y_{-})^{-1}G(Y_{-})

\displaystyle-A_{1}(Y)\partial_{Y}A_{2}(Y_{+})\partial_{Y}\eta(Y_{+})^{-1}G(Y_{+}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{2,1}^{(2)}(Y)=

\displaystyle A_{1}(Y)\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}\partial_{Z}^{2}A_{2}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}G(Z)dZ

\displaystyle-A_{1}(Y)\left(\int_{0}^{Y_{-}}+\int_{Y_{+}}^{Y}\right)\partial_{Z}A_{2}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}\partial_{Z}G(Z)dZ

\displaystyle-A_{2}(Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Z}A_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}\partial_{Z}G(Z)dZ,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}^{(2)}(Y)=

\displaystyle A_{1}(Y)\int_{0}^{Y}A_{2}(Z)\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{2}}\partial_{Z}G(Z)dZ-A_{1}(Y)\int_{\mathcal{N}}\partial_{Z}A_{2}(Z)\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{2}}G(Z)dZ

\displaystyle+A_{2}(Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}A_{1}(Z)\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{2}}\partial_{Z}G(Z)dZ.

Estimates about
\mathcal{M}_{1,1}^{(2)}(Y)
.
By Lemma
3.4
and a similar argument as in the calculation of
\mathcal{M}_{1}(Y)
, we can obtain that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,Y\geq Y_{c}\}
,

(3.43)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\mathcal{M}_{1,1}^{(2)}(Y)|\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}e^{-\theta Y}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}})\end{split}

Estimates about
\mathcal{M}_{2,1}^{(2)}(Y)
.

For any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,Y\geq Y_{c}\}
, we have

(3.44)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\mathcal{M}_{2,1}^{(2)}(Y)|\\
&\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-1}\int_{\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|\partial_{Z}G(Z)|dZ\\
&\quad+C|\varepsilon|^{-1}\int_{\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}||\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|\partial_{Z}G(Z)|dZ\\
&\quad+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{4}{3}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ.\end{split}

By Lemma
3.1
, we have that for
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,Y\geq Y_{c}\}
,

(3.45)

\displaystyle|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}}\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} &1,\quad Z\in\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y),\\
&|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}},\quad Z\in\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y).\end{aligned}\right.

Therefore, by a similar calculation of
\mathcal{M}_{2}(Y)
, we have that for
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,Y\geq Y_{c}\}
,

(3.46)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int_{\mathcal{N}_{near}(Y)}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|\partial_{Z}G(Z)|dZ\\
&\quad\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}},\end{split}

and

(3.47)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int_{\mathcal{N}_{far}(Y)}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}||\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|\partial_{Z}G(Z)|dZ\\
&\quad\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

For the last term on the right hand side of (
3.44
), we notice that if
Y-Y_{c}\geq L
,

\displaystyle\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-C|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|u(Z)-\hat{c}|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{2}}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}},

and for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,0\leq Y-Y_{c}\leq L\}
,

\displaystyle\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{7}{4}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}|G(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}},

which implies that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,Y\geq Y_{c}\}
,

(3.48)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int_{Z\in\mathcal{N}}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}|e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

Therefore by (
3.44
), (
3.46
), (
3.47
) and (
3.48
), we infer that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,Y\geq Y_{c}\}
,

(3.49)

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\mathcal{M}_{2,1}^{(2)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

By a similar argument as in the calculation of
\mathcal{E}^{(1)}_{j}
, we can obtain that for any
|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{E}^{(2)}_{1}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(1+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1})e^{-\theta Y}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

which along with (
3.42
), (
3.43
) and (
3.49
) deduces that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,Y\geq Y_{c}\}
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{app,s}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

By a similar argument, we can also infer that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\geq M+1,Y\leq Y_{c}\}
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{app,s}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

To show the estimates about
(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{app,s}(Y)
for the case of
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
, we notice that

(3.50)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{app,s}(Y)=&\mathcal{M}_{1,2}^{(2)}(Y)+\mathcal{M}_{2,2}^{(2)}(Y)+\mathcal{E}^{(2)}(Y),\end{split}

with

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1,2}^{(2)}(Y)=

\displaystyle-A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(0)\partial_{Y}\eta(0)^{-1}\partial_{Y}G(0)+A_{1}(Y)\partial_{Y}A_{2}(0)\partial_{Y}\eta(0)^{-1}G(0),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{2,2}^{(2)}(Y)=

\displaystyle A_{1}(Y)\int_{0}^{Y}\partial_{Z}^{2}A_{2}\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}G(Z)dZ+A_{2}(Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\partial_{Z}^{2}A_{1}\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}G(Z)dZ,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(2)}(Y)=

\displaystyle A_{1}(Y)\int_{0}^{Y}\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{2}}(A_{2}(Z)\partial_{Z}G(Z)-\partial_{Z}A_{2}(Z)G(Z))dZ

\displaystyle+A_{2}(Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta(Z)}{\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{2}}(A_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}G(Z)-\partial_{Z}A_{1}(Z)G(Z))dZ.

Again by Lemma
3.4
and a similar argument of the calculation of
\mathcal{M}_{1}(Y)
, we can obtain that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
,

(3.51)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\mathcal{M}_{1,2}^{(2)}(Y)|\leq&C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{c}|^{-1}+|\hat{c}|^{-2})\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&+C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{c}|^{-1})\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
\leq&C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.\end{split}

Estimates about
\mathcal{M}^{(2)}_{2,2}(Y)
for
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
.

By Lemma
3.4
, we have that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\mathcal{M}_{2,2}^{(2)}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{4}{3}}\int_{Z\in\{|\kappa\eta(Z)|\geq M+2\}}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{3}{4}}|\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)|e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{4}{3}}\int_{Z\in\{|\kappa\eta(Z)|\leq M+2\}}|u(Y)-\hat{c}|^{2}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|G(Z)|dZ=\mathcal{II}_{1}+\mathcal{II}_{2}.

About
\mathcal{II}_{1}
, we notice that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{II}_{1}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-1}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\{|\kappa\eta(Z)|\geq M+2\}}|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{3}{4}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

About
\mathcal{II}_{2}
, we notice that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{II}_{2}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{Z\in\{|\kappa\eta(Z)|\leq M+2\}}|u(Z)-\hat{c}|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Therefore, we obtain that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
,

(3.52)

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\mathcal{M}_{2,2}^{(2)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Again by a similar argument as in the calculation of
\mathcal{E}^{(1)}_{j}
, we can obtain that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(2)}_{2}(Y)\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}(|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||+|c||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

which along with(
3.50
), (
3.51
) and (
3.52
) deduces that for any
Y\in\{Y:|\kappa\eta(Y)|\leq M+1\}
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{app,s}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Therefore, we obtain that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{app,s}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||e^{-\theta Y}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Estimates about
\partial_{Y}w_{app,s}(Y)
.
By the definition of
w_{app,s}
, we have that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}w_{app,s}(Y)=\mathcal{M}_{1}^{(3)}(Y)+\mathcal{M}_{2}^{(3)}(Y)+\mathcal{E}^{(3)}(Y),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{(3)}_{1}(Y)=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{-1}\partial_{Y}G(Y)-\partial_{Y}A_{1}(Y)A_{2}(0)\partial_{Y}\eta(0)^{-1}\partial_{Y}G(0),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{(3)}_{2}(Y)=

\displaystyle-\partial_{Y}A_{1}(Y)\int_{0}^{Y}\partial_{Z}A_{2}\partial_{Z}\eta^{-1}\partial_{Z}GdZ-\partial_{Y}A_{2}(Y)\int_{0}^{Y}\partial_{Z}A_{1}\partial_{Z}\eta^{-1}\partial_{Z}GdZ,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(3)}(Y)=

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}A_{1}(Y)\int_{0}^{Y}A_{2}(Z)\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta}{(\partial_{Z}\eta)^{2}}\partial_{Z}GdZ+\partial_{Y}A_{2}(Y)\int_{0}^{Y}A_{1}(Z)\frac{\partial_{Z}^{2}\eta}{(\partial_{Z}\eta)^{2}}\partial_{Z}GdZ.

Firstly, we notice that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{-1}|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}G(Y)|\leq Ce^{-\theta Y}|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}},

which along with a similar argument as in
\mathcal{M}^{(1)}_{1}(Y)
implies that for any
Y\geq 0
,

(3.53)

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{1}^{(3)}(Y)|\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}|^{-2})e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Again by Lemma
3.4
and a similar calculation of
\mathcal{M}_{2}(Y)
, we can obtain that for any
Y\geq 0
,

(3.54)

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{M}_{2}^{(3)}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{4}{3}}+|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1})e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

By a similar argument as above, we can obtain that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\mathcal{E}^{(3)}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C(|\varepsilon|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1})e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}},

which along with (
3.53
) and (
3.54
) deduces that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}w_{app,s}(Y)|\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{4}{3}})e^{-\theta Y}\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

The proof is completed.
∎

Corollary 3.1
.

Under the same assumptions in Proposition
3.7
,
\psi_{app,s}(Y)
defined by (
3.10
) satisfies that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|e^{\theta Y}(u-\hat{c})Err_{1}(Y)w_{app,s}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

\displaystyle|e^{\theta Y}(u-\hat{c})Err_{2}(Y)\partial_{Y}w_{app,s}(Y)|\leq C(|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Proof.

By Lemma
3.2
, we have for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|Err_{1}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|u(Y)-c|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|u-\hat{c}|,

which implies that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|e^{\theta_{0}Y}(u-\hat{c})Err_{1}(Y)w_{app,s}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|(u-\hat{c})^{2}w_{app,s}(Y)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

We also notice that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|Err_{2}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|,

which implies that for any
Y\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|e^{\theta_{0}Y}(u-\hat{c})Err_{2}(Y)\partial_{Y}w_{app,s}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon||(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}w_{app,s}(Y)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

The proof is completed.
∎

By the construction of
\psi_{app,s}
in Proposition
3.7
, we can obtain the following estimates for the boundary value of
\psi_{app,s}
and
\partial_{Y}\psi_{app,s}
.

Corollary 3.2
.

Under the same assumptions in Proposition
3.7
, the boundary value of the solution
\psi_{app,s}(Y)
defined by (
3.10
) satisfies

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{app,s}(0)-u^{\prime}(0)^{-1}c_{r}\mathcal{M}_{0}\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}\psi_{app,s}(0)+\mathcal{M}_{0}\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}|^{-1}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]:=\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}G(Z)dZ.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Proof.

By the definition of
\psi_{app,s}(Y)
, we know that

\displaystyle\psi_{app,s}(0)=\sum_{j=1}^{4}\mathcal{M}_{j}(0)+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(0)}(0).

According to the calculation in the proof of Proposition
3.7
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{app,s}(0)-\mathcal{M}_{3}(0)\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

We also notice that

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{M}_{3}(0)-Y_{c}\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]\right|=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}(Z-Y_{c})\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}G(Z)dZ\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

provided by a similar argument as in the proof of Proposition
3.7
.
Then we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{app,s}(0)-Y_{c}\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

which along with the fact
Y_{c}=u^{\prime}(0)^{-1}c_{r}+\mathcal{O}(|\hat{c}|^{2})

implies that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{app,s}(0)-u^{\prime}(0)^{-1}c_{r}\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

By the definition of
\partial_{Y}\psi_{app,s}(Y)
, we know that

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\psi_{app,s}(0)=\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]+\mathcal{M}_{1}^{(1)}(0)+\mathcal{M}_{2}^{(1)}(0)+\mathcal{E}^{(1)}_{1}(0)+\mathcal{E}^{(1)}_{2}(0),

which along with the arguments in Proposition
3.7
deduces that

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}\psi_{app,s}(0)+\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]\right|\leq C|\hat{c}|^{-1}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Moreover, by a similar calculation as in Proposition
3.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

The proof is completed.
∎

3.4.
Non-homogeneous Airy equation

In this part, we are aim to construct a solution to the Airy equation (
3.1
) with several types of source terms
F
. By a similar argument as in the proof of Proposition 4.7 in
[
27
]
, we can obtain the following result.

Theorem 3.8
.

Let
1\lesssim\theta\leq 3\eta_{0}
and
\hat{c}:=c+\mathrm{i}c_{0}
with
c_{0}\in(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}},|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})
. Suppose
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{2}
with
c_{i}>-c_{0}/2
. Then there exists a solution
\psi\in W^{4,\infty}
to (
3.1
) satisfies the following properties:

•

for any
e^{\theta Y}F\in L^{\infty}
, we have

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|\|\partial^{2}_{Y}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\partial_{Y}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C\|F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}},

\displaystyle\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|\partial_{Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}.

•

for any
e^{\theta Y}(u-\hat{c})F\in L^{\infty}
, we have

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{2}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}|\|(u-\hat{c})F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}},

\displaystyle\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\hat{c}||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}.

Now, we present the results for the singular source term corresponding to the sublayer.

Theorem 3.9
.

Under the same assumptions in Proposition
3.7
, there is a solution to (
3.1
) with the source term
F(Y)=\partial_{Y}^{2}G(Y)
satisfying the following properties

\displaystyle\|\psi_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{c}|\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}w_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}})|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}w_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{W}(Y)\partial^{2}_{Y}w_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq

\displaystyle C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}})\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})^{3}\partial_{Y}^{2}G\|_{L^{\infty}},

where
\mathcal{W}(Y)>0
is a smooth weighted function defined such that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}
,
\mathcal{W}(Y)\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|u-\hat{c}|^{3}
and for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}\cup\mathcal{N}^{+}
,
\mathcal{W}(Y)\sim|\varepsilon||u-\hat{c}|
.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle|\psi_{s}(0)-u^{\prime}(0)^{-1}c_{r}\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}\psi_{s}(0)+\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]|\leq|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}|^{-1}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

with

\displaystyle|\mathcal{M}_{0}[G]|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-1}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Proof.

We first define
w_{s}^{(0)}(Y):=w_{app,s}(Y)
, where
w_{app,s}(Y)
is the solution to (
3.9
) constructed in Proposition
3.7
. Then we know that

\displaystyle Airy[w_{app,s}](Y)=\partial_{Y}^{2}G(Y)+Err_{1}(Y)w_{app,s}(Y)+Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{app,s}(Y).

Moreover, by Proposition
3.7
and Corollary
3.1
, we have

(3.55)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathcal{W}(Y)\partial^{2}_{Y}w_{app,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq&C\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}w_{app,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+C\|(u-\hat{c})^{3}\partial_{Y}^{2}G\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&+\|(u-\hat{c})Err_{1}w_{app,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{app,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\\
\leq&C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}})|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})^{3}\partial_{Y}^{2}G\|_{L^{\infty}},\end{split}

where
\mathcal{W}(Y)>0
is a smooth weighted function defined such that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}
,
\mathcal{W}(Y)\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|u-\hat{c}|^{3}
and for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}\cup\mathcal{N}^{+}
,
\mathcal{W}(Y)\sim|\varepsilon||u-\hat{c}|
.

Now we define
(w_{s,err}(Y),\psi_{s,err}(Y))
to be the solution of

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{s,err}-(u-c)w_{s,err}=-Err_{1}w_{s}^{(0)}-Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{s}^{(0)},

\displaystyle(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{s,err}(Y)=w_{s,err}(Y),\quad\lim_{Y\to\infty}\psi_{s,err}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}w_{s,err}(Y)=0,

constructed in Theorem
3.8
. Then we first have

\displaystyle Airy[w^{(0)}_{s}+w_{s,err}](Y)=\partial_{Y}^{2}G.

By Corollary
3.1
and Theorem
3.8
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}w_{s,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}w_{s,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{2}w_{s,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})Err_{1}w_{app,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{app,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}})

\displaystyle\leq C(|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||^{2}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Similarly, we can obtain

(3.56)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\psi_{s,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{s,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\\
&\quad\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||^{3}(|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).\end{split}

By Proposition
3.7
, (
3.55
) and the above estimates, we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{\psi}_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\bar{\psi}_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}||\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}},

\displaystyle|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}w_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}})(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}w_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}),

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{W}(Y)\partial^{2}_{Y}w_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq

\displaystyle C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1}+|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}})\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})^{3}\partial_{Y}^{2}G\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Here
\bar{\psi}_{s}:=\psi_{app,s}+\psi_{s,err}
.

Now we are left with the construction of
\psi_{s}(Y)
. By the definitions of
\psi_{app,s}
and
\psi_{s,err}
, we have

\displaystyle(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})(\psi_{app,s}+\psi_{s,err})=w_{s}-\alpha^{2}\psi_{app,s}.

Hence, we can construct
\psi_{s,1}
by the iteration such that

\displaystyle(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{s,1}=\alpha^{2}\psi_{app,s},\quad\psi_{app,1}(\infty)=0,

and

(3.57)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\psi_{s,1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{s,1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C\alpha^{2}\|e^{\theta_{0}\eta_{r}(Y)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\psi_{s,app}\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\quad\leq C\alpha^{2}(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}+|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}|)|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}).\end{split}

Therefore, by defining
\psi_{s}:=\psi_{app,s}+\psi_{s,err}+\psi_{s,1}
, we have that
(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{s}=w_{s}
and

\displaystyle\|\psi_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}+|\varepsilon|^{-1}|\hat{c}|)|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

Moreover, by the definition of
\psi_{s}
and (
3.56
) and (
3.57
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\psi_{s}-\psi_{app,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}(\psi_{s}-\psi_{app,s})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(\|(u-\hat{c})G\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}G\|_{L^{\infty}}).

The proof is completed.
∎

3.5.
Homogeneous Airy equation

In this part, we construct a non-zero solution to the homogeneous Airy equation

(3.58)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{a}-(u-c)w_{a}=0\quad,(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{a}(Y)=w_{a}(Y),\\
&\lim_{Y\to\infty}\psi_{a}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}w_{a}(Y)=0.\end{split}

We denote

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,Y):=-\int_{Y}^{+\infty}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta(Z))dZ\text{ and }\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,Y):=-\int_{Y}^{+\infty}\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(1,Z)dZ.

We also define

\displaystyle w_{a}^{(0)}(Y):=\frac{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta(Y))}{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta(0))}\text{ and }\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)=\frac{\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2,Y)}{Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta(0))}.

By (
3.4
), we know that

\displaystyle Airy[w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)]=Err_{1}(Y)w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)+Err_{2}(Y)\partial_{Y}w^{(0)}_{a}(Y).

To eliminate the above errors, we define
w_{a,err}(Y)
as the solution constructed in Theorem
3.8
with source term
-Err_{1}(Y)w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)-Err_{2}(Y)\partial_{Y}w^{(0)}_{a}(Y)
. We also show that the boundary value of
\psi_{a,err}
is a small perturbation compared with
\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)
. Now we state our results.

Theorem 3.10
.

Let
1\lesssim\theta\leq 3\eta_{0}
and
\hat{c}:=c+\mathrm{i}c_{0}
with
c_{0}\in(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}},|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})
. Suppose
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{2}
with
c_{i}>-c_{0}/2
. Then there exists a solution
\psi_{a}(Y)\in W^{4,\infty}
to (
3.58
) satisfying

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}(w_{a}-w_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}(w_{a}-w_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{2}(w_{a}-w_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|c|^{2})|c_{i}|,

\displaystyle\|(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|c_{i}c|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\alpha^{2}).

Proof.

By the definition of
A_{1}(2,Y)
and the properties of Airy function, we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)=w_{a}^{(0)}(Y),

and

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{a}^{(0)}-(u-c)w_{a}^{(0)}=Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}+Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}.

Hence, to construct a homogeneous solution to the Airy equation (
3.58
), we need to construct a solution
(w_{a,err},\psi_{a,err})
to

(3.59)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{a,err}-(u-c)w_{a,err}=-Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}-Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)},\\
&(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{a,err}(Y)=w_{a,err}(Y)-\alpha^{2}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y),\\
&\lim_{Y\to\infty}\psi_{a,err}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}w_{a,err}(Y)=0.\end{split}

Case 1.
0\in\mathcal{N}
.

We notice that if
0\in\mathcal{N}
, then
\mathcal{N}^{-}=\emptyset
and
|Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta(0))|\leq C
. Moreover, since
\mathrm{Im}(\kappa\eta(0))<\delta_{0}
, there exists
C_{1}
independent of
\varepsilon
such that
|Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta(0))|\geq C_{1}>0
, which along with Lemma
3.4
implies that

(3.60)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq Ce^{-C|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}},\quad|\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}},\\
&|\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\text{ and }|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-C|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{3}{2}}}.\end{split}

Moreover, we have the following controls on the boundary value of
\psi_{a}^{(0)}
and
\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}
:

\displaystyle|\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\text{ and }|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}.

Therefore, we obtain

(3.61)

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|.

Hence, by Theorem
3.8
, there exists a solution
(w_{a,err},\psi_{a,err})
to (
3.59
) satisfying

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{2}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||\varepsilon|,

and

\displaystyle\|\psi_{a,err}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||^{2}|\varepsilon|,

where
(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{a,err}^{(1)}=w_{a,err}
. By a similar argument as in the proof of Theorem
3.9
, we can construct a solution
\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}
to
(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}=-\alpha^{2}\psi^{(0)}_{a}
satisfying

\displaystyle\|\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\alpha|^{2}|\varepsilon|.

Then by denoting
\psi_{a,err}(Y):=\psi_{a,err}^{(1)}(Y)+\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}(Y)
, we construct a solution to (
3.59
) and

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{2}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||(|c_{i}|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}},

and

\displaystyle\|\psi_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||^{2}(|c_{i}|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}.

As a consequence, we have

\displaystyle\|\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||^{2}(|c_{i}|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}},

which implies that

\displaystyle\psi_{a}(0)=\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||^{2}(|c_{i}|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}),\quad\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}(0)=\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}).

Case 2.
0\in\mathcal{N}^{-}
.

In this case, by Lemma
3.3
, we have that for
k=0,1
,

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\sim|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{k}{3}}|\partial_{Y}^{k}Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta(Y))Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{5\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta(0))|,

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2-k}{3}}|\tilde{\mathcal{A}}(2-k,Y)Ai(e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{5\pi}{6}}\kappa\eta(0))|,

which along with Lemma
3.4
implies that for any
k=0,1
,

(3.62)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{k}{3}}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}|\kappa\eta(0)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta(Y)-\eta(0)|(|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}})},\\
&\left|\partial_{Y}^{k}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(Y)\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2-k}{3}}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{-\frac{5}{4}+\frac{k}{2}}|\kappa\eta(0)|^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta(Y)-\eta(0)|(|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\kappa\eta(0)|^{\frac{1}{2}})}.\end{split}

In particular, we have

\displaystyle|\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\kappa\eta(0)|^{-1}\sim|\varepsilon||c|^{-1},\quad|\partial_{Y}\psi_{a}^{(0)}(0)|\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\kappa\eta(0)|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}|c|^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

To show the estimates for the solution to (
3.59
), we need to show the control of
Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)
and
Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)
. By Lemma
3.2
and (
3.62
), we can obtain that for any
|Y|\geq|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}
,

(3.63)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|(u-\hat{c})Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|e^{-\theta_{0}\eta_{r}(Y)^{\frac{3}{2}}},\\
&|(u-\hat{c})Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|e^{-\theta_{0}\eta_{r}(Y)^{\frac{3}{2}}},\end{split}

and for
Y\in[0,|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}]
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|c|^{2}e^{-\theta_{0}\eta_{r}(Y)^{\frac{3}{2}}},

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}(Y)|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|c|e^{-\theta_{0}\eta_{r}(Y)^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

As a consequence, we obtain

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|c|^{2}),\quad\|(u-\hat{c})Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|c|).

Therefore, by Theorem
3.8
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+|\varepsilon|\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{2}w_{a,err}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C|\log|\varepsilon||(|c|^{2}|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+|c\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}).

However, the bounds of the corresponding stream function
\psi_{a,err}
deduced by Theorem
3.8
are not small enough to make it as a small perturbation of
\psi_{a}^{(0)}
when the frequency is near the upper branch of the neutral curve. Hence, our next task to provide sharper estimates for
\psi_{a,err}(Y)
. For this purpose, we introduce the following notations

\displaystyle F_{1}:=\mathbf{1}_{[|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},+\infty)}(Y)(-Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}-Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}),

\displaystyle F_{2}:=\mathbf{1}_{[0,|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}]}(Y)(-Err_{1}w_{a}^{(0)}-Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a}^{(0)}).

Let
(w_{a,err}^{(1)},\psi_{a,err}^{(1)})
be the solution to (
3.1
) with source term
F_{1}
constructed as in Theorem
3.8
and
(w_{a,err}^{(2)},\psi_{a,err}^{(2)})
be defined by (
3.6
) with source term
F_{2}
.
Moreover, we define
(w_{a,err}^{(3)},\psi_{a,err}^{(3)})
to be the solution to the following system

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})w_{a,err}^{(3)}-(u-c)w_{a,err}^{(3)}=-Err_{1}w_{a,err}^{(2)}-Err_{2}\partial_{Y}w_{a,err}^{(2)},

\displaystyle(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{a,err}^{(3)}(Y)=w_{a,err}^{(3)}(Y)-\alpha^{2}\psi_{a}^{(0)}-\alpha^{2}\psi_{a,err}^{(2)},

\displaystyle\lim_{Y\to\infty}\psi_{a,err}^{(3)}(Y)=\lim_{Y\to\infty}w_{a,err}^{(3)}(Y)=0.

Firstly, by Theorem
3.8
and (
3.63
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|e^{\theta_{0}\eta_{r}(Y)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\psi_{a,err}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\theta_{0}\eta_{r}(Y)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||^{2}|\varepsilon|.

For the estimates of
\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}
, by the definition, we can write

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}(Y)=

\displaystyle A_{1}(1,Y)\int_{0}^{Y}A_{2}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}F_{2}(Z)dZ+A_{2}(1,Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}A_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta^{-1}(Z)F_{2}(Z)dZ

\displaystyle+\int_{Y}^{+\infty}[A_{1}(1,Z)A_{2}(Z)-A_{2}(1,Z)A_{1}(Z)]\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}F_{2}(Z)dZ.

For any
Y\in[|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},+\infty)
, notice that

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}(Y)=

\displaystyle A_{1}(1,Y)\int_{0}^{Y}A_{2}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}F_{2}(Z)dZ+A_{2}(1,Y)\int_{Y}^{+\infty}A_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta^{-1}(Z)F_{2}(Z)dZ,

which implies that for any
Y\in[|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},+\infty)
,

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}(Y)|\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\partial_{Y}\eta(Y)^{-1}||\kappa\eta(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|F_{2}(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}e^{-\theta Y}\int_{0}^{|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}}1dZ\leq C|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}e^{-\theta Y}.

On the other hand, for any
Y\in[0,|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}]
, we have

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}(Y)|

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{1}{4}}||\kappa\eta(Z)|^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-W_{\varepsilon}(Y,Z)}|F_{2}(Z)|dZ+C|u-\hat{c}||\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\int_{0}^{|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}}|\kappa\eta(Z)|^{-1}|F_{2}(Z)|dZ

\displaystyle\leq C|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\int_{0}^{|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}}|\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\gamma_{0}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\eta(Y)-\eta(Z)||\kappa\eta(Y)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}dZ+C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|c|\int_{0}^{|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}}1dZ\leq C|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}.

Therefore, we obtain

(3.64)

\displaystyle\|e^{\theta Y}(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}},

which implies that

(3.65)

\displaystyle\|e^{\theta Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||.

Now we are left with the estimates for
\psi_{a,err}^{(3)}
. Let
\psi_{a,err}^{(3,1)}
be the solution to
(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{a,err}^{(3,1)}=w_{a,err}^{(3)}
and
\psi_{a,err}^{(3,2)}
be the solution to
(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{a,err}^{(3,2)}=-\alpha^{2}\psi_{a}^{(0)}-\alpha^{2}\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}
. Then by Proposition
3.6
Theorem
3.8
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\psi_{a,err}^{(3,1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(3,1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||^{3}(|c_{i}|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|c_{i}|(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}+|c|^{2})\leq C|c||\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}},

and

\displaystyle\|\psi_{a,err}^{(3,2)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|e^{\theta_{0}\eta_{r}(Y)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\partial_{Y}\psi_{a,err}^{(3,2)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C\alpha^{2}(\|\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|\psi_{a}^{(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}})\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\alpha^{2}.

Then by defining
w_{a,err}:=w_{a,err}^{(1)}+w_{a,err}^{(2)}+w_{a,err}^{(3)}
and
\psi_{a,err}=\psi_{a,err}^{(1)}+\psi_{a,err}^{(2)}+\psi_{a,err}^{(3,1)}+\psi_{a,err}^{(3,2)}
, we construct a solution to (
3.59
). Hence,
w_{a}:=w_{a}^{(0)}+w_{a,err}
and
\psi_{a}:=\psi_{a}^{(0)}+\psi_{a,err}
are a solution to (
3.58
) and

\displaystyle\|(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|c_{i}c|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\alpha^{2}),\quad\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}(\psi_{a}-\psi_{a}^{(0)})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}(|c_{i}c|+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\alpha^{2}).

The proof is completed.
∎

4.
The Orr-Sommerfeld equation

This section is devoted to constructing the slow mode
\phi_{s}
and the fast mode
\phi_{f}
for the following homogeneous Orr-Semmerfeld equation:

(4.1)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})^{2}\phi-(u-c)(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi+u^{\prime\prime}\phi=0,\\
&\lim_{Y\to\infty}\phi(Y)=0.\end{aligned}\right.

The leading order terms of the slow mode and the fast mode are the solution
\varphi_{Ray}
to the modified homogeneous Rayleigh equation and the solution
\psi_{a}
to the homogeneous Airy equation, respectively. We develop a modified Rayleigh-Airy iteration to construct the exact solutions from
\varphi_{Ray}
and
\psi_{a}
around the neutral curve.

Let us introduce that following function spaces. We define
\hat{c}=c+\mathrm{i}|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}
and

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}:=\{f\in W^{4,\infty}|\|f\|_{\mathcal{X}}<+\infty\},

where

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\mathcal{X}}:=

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{W}\partial_{Y}^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{3}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}.

Here
\mathcal{W}(Y)>0
is a smooth weighted function defined such that for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}
,
\mathcal{W}(Y)\sim|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}|u-\hat{c}|^{3}
and for any
Y\in\mathcal{N}^{-}\cup\mathcal{N}^{+}
,
\mathcal{W}(Y)\sim|\varepsilon||u-\hat{c}|
. We also define

\displaystyle\mathcal{Y}:=\{f\in W^{4,\infty}|\|f\|_{\mathcal{Y}}<+\infty\},

where

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\mathcal{Y}}=

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{3}\partial_{Y}^{4}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{3}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}.

In the rest of this section, we always assume
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{2}\cap\{(\alpha,c):\alpha\sim c_{r}\}:=\mathbb{H}_{3}
.

4.1.
Non-homogeneous OS equation

In this part, we consider the following non-homogeneous OS equation for
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{3}
:

(4.2)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})^{2}\phi_{non}-(u-c)(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\phi_{non}+u^{\prime\prime}\phi_{non}=f,\\
&\lim_{Y\to\infty}\phi_{non}(Y)=0.\end{aligned}\right.

We develop a modified Rayleigh-Airy iteration to construct a solution of (
4.2
).

Theorem 4.1
.

Let
\hat{c}=c+\mathrm{i}c_{0}
with
|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\ll c_{0}\ll|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}
and
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{3}
with
c_{i}>-c_{0}/2
. Then for any
e^{\eta_{0}Y}f\in W^{2,\infty}
, there exists a solution
\phi_{non}\in\mathcal{X}
to (
4.2
) satisfying

\displaystyle\|\phi_{non}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\log|\varepsilon||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}).

Proof.

The initial step of the iteration.

We introduce that
\varphi^{(1)}
, as constructed in Proposition
2.1
, is the solution to the following non-homogeneous Rayleigh equation

\displaystyle Ray_{\hat{c}}[\varphi^{(1)}]=-f,\quad\varphi^{(1)}(\infty)=0.

Then by Proposition
2.1
, we have

(4.3)

\displaystyle\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C|\log|\varepsilon||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}),

and

(4.4)

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}^{2}\varphi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\partial_{Y}\varphi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C|\log|\varepsilon||\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}.

Moreover, we notice that

\displaystyle OS[\varphi^{(1)}]=

\displaystyle f+\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})^{2}\varphi^{(1)}-\mathrm{i}c_{0}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f+\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(1)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{f}{u-\hat{c}}\Big)-(\mathrm{i}c_{0}+\varepsilon\alpha^{2})\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(1)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{f}{u-\hat{c}}\Big).

Next we introduce
\psi^{(1)}=\psi_{1}^{(1)}+\psi_{2}^{(1)}+\psi_{3}^{(1)}
, where
\psi_{j}^{(1)}
for
j=1,2,3
satisfy

\displaystyle Airy[(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{1}^{(1)}]=-\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}\Big[(1-\chi)\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(1)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{f}{u-\hat{c}}\Big)\Big],

\displaystyle Airy[(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{2}^{(1)}]=(\mathrm{i}c_{0}+\varepsilon\alpha^{2})\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(1)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{f}{u-\hat{c}}\Big),

\displaystyle Airy[(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{3}^{(1)}]=-\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}\Big[\chi\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(1)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{f}{u-\hat{c}}\Big)\Big].

Here
\chi(Y)
is a smooth cut-off function with
\chi(Y)\equiv 1
on
[0,\frac{1}{2}]
and
\chi(Y)\equiv 0
on
[1,+\infty)
. According to Theorem
3.8
and (
4.4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\psi_{1}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon||\log|\varepsilon||(\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}})\leq C|\varepsilon||\log|\varepsilon||^{2}\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}},

\displaystyle\|\psi_{2}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\alpha c_{0}|\log|\varepsilon||(\|\varphi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}})\leq C|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}\alpha c_{0}|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}.

By Theorem
3.9
and (
4.3
), we have

\displaystyle\|\psi_{3}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C(\alpha+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1})|\log|\varepsilon||\Big\|(u-\hat{c})^{3}\partial_{Y}^{2}\Big[\chi\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(1)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{f}{u-\hat{c}}\Big)\Big]\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C(\alpha+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1})|\log|\varepsilon||\Big\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}\Big[\chi\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(1)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{f}{u-\hat{c}}\Big)\Big]\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+C(\alpha+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1})|\log|\varepsilon||\Big\|(u-\hat{c})\chi\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(1)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{f}{u-\hat{c}}\Big)\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\alpha+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1})|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}),

and

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{3}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{3}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\psi_{3}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}).

Therefore, we obtain

(4.5)

\displaystyle\|\psi^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C(\alpha+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1})|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}),

and

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\psi^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}

(4.6)

\displaystyle\leq C\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}).

We define
\phi^{(1)}=\varphi^{(1)}+\psi^{(1)}
, which satisfies

(4.7)

\displaystyle OS[\phi^{(1)}]=f+u^{\prime\prime}\psi^{(1)}.

The iteration scheme.
For any
j\geq 2
, we define
\phi^{(j)}=\varphi^{(j)}+\psi^{(j)}
, where
\varphi^{(j)}
is the solution to the following non-homogeneous Rayleigh equation,

\displaystyle Ray_{\hat{c}}[\varphi^{(j)}]=u^{\prime\prime}\psi^{(j-1)},\quad\varphi^{(j)}(\infty)=0,

and
\psi^{(j)}=\psi_{1}^{(j)}+\psi_{2}^{(j)}+\psi_{3}^{(j)}
with
\psi_{1}^{(j)},\psi_{2}^{(j)},\psi_{3}^{(j)}
satisfying the following non-homogeneous Airy equations, respectively

(4.8)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&Airy[(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{1}^{(j)}]=-\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}\Big[(1-\chi)\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(j)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\psi^{(j-1)}}{u-\hat{c}}\Big)\Big],\\
&Airy[(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{2}^{(j)}]=(\mathrm{i}c_{0}+\varepsilon\alpha^{2})\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(j)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\psi^{(j-1)}}{u-\hat{c}}\Big),\\
&Airy[(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{3}^{(j)}]=-\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}\Big[\chi\Big(\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\varphi^{(j)}}{u-\hat{c}}-\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\psi^{(j-1)}}{u-\hat{c}}\Big)\Big].\end{split}

By Proposition
2.1
, Theorem
3.9
, Theorem
3.8
and a similar argument as above, we can obtain

(4.9)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\varphi^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\log|\varepsilon||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}),\\
&\|\psi^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C(\alpha+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}|\hat{c}_{i}|^{-1})|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\quad+\|\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}),\end{split}

and

(4.10)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\psi^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\\
&\leq C\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\psi^{(j-1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}).\end{split}

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle OS[\phi^{(j)}]=-u^{\prime\prime}\psi^{(j-1)}+u^{\prime\prime}\psi^{(j)}.

The construction of
\phi_{non}
.
We define
\phi_{non}=\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}\phi^{(j)}
, which satisfies
OS[\phi_{non}]=f
formally. Hence, we are left with the proof of the convergence of the series
\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}\phi^{(j)}
. By (
4.10
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\psi^{(j)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||^{2})^{j}(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}),

which along with (
4.9
) deduces

\displaystyle\|\phi^{(j)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C(\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||^{2})^{j-1}|\log|\varepsilon||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|\phi_{non}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log|\varepsilon||(\sum_{j=1}^{+\infty}(\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||^{2})^{j})(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\log|\varepsilon||(\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}).

The proof is completed. ∎

4.2.
The slow mode

In this part, we construct the slow mode of (
4.1
). Let
\varphi_{Ray}(Y)
be the solution to the homogeneous Rayleigh equation as constructed in Proposition
2.6
, with
c
replaced by
\hat{c}:=c+\mathrm{i}|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}
. That is,

(4.11)

\displaystyle(u-\hat{c})(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray}-u^{\prime\prime}\varphi_{Ray}=0,\quad\varphi_{Ray}(\infty)=0.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle OS[\varphi_{Ray}](Y)=

\displaystyle\varepsilon(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})^{2}\varphi_{Ray}-\mathrm{i}|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\varphi_{Ray}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}(u^{\prime\prime}(u-\hat{c})^{-1}\varphi_{Ray})-(\varepsilon\alpha^{2}+\mathrm{i}|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}})u^{\prime\prime}(u-\hat{c})^{-1}\varphi_{Ray}.

By Proposition
2.6
, we know that

\displaystyle\varphi_{Ray}=(u-\hat{c})e^{-\alpha Y}+\tilde{\varphi}_{R}(Y),\quad\|\tilde{\varphi}_{R}\|_{\mathcal{Y}}\leq C\alpha|\log\hat{c}_{i}|.

As a consequence, there exist
H_{1}(Y)\in L^{\infty},H_{2}(Y)\in W^{2,\infty}
such that

(4.12)

\displaystyle\frac{u^{\prime\prime}(Y)\varphi_{Ray}(Y)}{u(Y)-\hat{c}}=H_{1}(Y)+H_{2}(Y),

and

(4.13)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|H_{1}(Y)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C,\quad\|H_{1}-u^{\prime\prime}e^{-\alpha Y}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|H_{2}-\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})(u-\hat{c})}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C\alpha|\log\hat{c}_{i}|.\\
&\|(u-\hat{c})H_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}H_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{3}\partial_{Y}^{2}H_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C\alpha|\log\hat{c}_{i}|.\end{split}

Then we can write

\displaystyle OS[\varphi_{Ray}](Y)=\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}H_{1}(Y)+\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}H_{2}(Y)-(\mathrm{i}|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon\alpha^{2})(H_{1}+H_{2}).

Theorem 4.2
.

Let
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{3}
with
c_{i}>-|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}/2
. Then there exists a solution
\phi_{s}
to (
4.1
) such that
e^{\alpha Y}\phi_{s}(Y)\in W^{4,\infty}(\mathbb{R}_{+})
and

\displaystyle\phi_{s}(0)=-c+u^{\prime}(0)^{-1}\alpha+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha^{2})|\log|\varepsilon||^{3},

\displaystyle\partial_{Y}\phi_{s}(0)=u^{\prime}(0)+\mathcal{O}(|\alpha||\log|\varepsilon||).

Moreover, if
\alpha\gg|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{4}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Im}(\phi_{s}(0))=-c_{i}+\frac{\alpha c_{r}u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})\pi}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{3}}+o(\alpha^{2}),\quad\mathrm{Im}(\partial_{Y}\phi_{s}(0))=-\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})\pi}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{2}}+o(\alpha).

Proof.

Let
\varphi_{Ray}
be the solution to (
4.11
). We define
\psi^{(1)}_{s}=\psi_{1,s}^{(1)}+\psi_{2,s}^{(1)}+\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}
, where
\psi_{1,s}^{(1)},\psi_{2,s}^{(1)}
and
\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}
satisfy the following equations, respectively

(4.14)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&Airy[(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{1,s}^{(1)}]=-\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}H_{1}-\varepsilon(\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}-\alpha^{2})H_{1}-\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}[(1-\chi)H_{2}]\\
&Airy[(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{2,s}^{(1)}]=-\varepsilon(\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}-\alpha^{2})H_{2},\quad Airy[(\partial_{Y}^{2}-\alpha^{2})\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}]=-\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}(\chi H_{2}).\end{split}

Here
\chi(Y)
is a smooth cut-off function with
\chi(Y)\equiv 1
on
[0,\frac{1}{2}]
and
\chi(Y)\equiv 0
on
[1,+\infty)
.
Hence, by (
4.13
)

\displaystyle\|\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}H_{1}+\varepsilon(\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}-\alpha^{2})H_{1}+\varepsilon\partial_{Y}^{2}[(1-\chi)H_{2}]\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}},

which along with Theorem
3.8
implies that

(4.15)

\displaystyle\|\psi_{1,s}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}|\log|\varepsilon||.

In particular,

(4.16)

\displaystyle|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi^{(1)}_{1,s}(0)|+|\psi_{1,s}^{(1)}(0)|\leq C|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}|\log|\varepsilon||.

Again by (
4.13
), we have

\displaystyle\|(u-\hat{c})\varepsilon(\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}-\alpha^{2})H_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{5}{6}}|\log|\hat{c}_{i}||,

which along with Theorem
3.8
implies that

(4.17)

\displaystyle\|\psi_{2,s}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||^{3}.

By the definition of
\mathbb{H}_{3}
, (
4.13
) and Theorem
3.9
, we obtain

(4.18)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|\varepsilon|^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(u-\hat{c})\partial^{3}_{Y}\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|\mathcal{W}(Y)\partial^{4}_{Y}\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\\
&\leq C\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}(\alpha^{\frac{2}{3}}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}+\alpha^{2}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})\leq C\alpha^{\frac{5}{2}}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log|\varepsilon||,\\
&\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||,\\
&\|\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\alpha^{2}|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}.\end{split}

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\|\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq\alpha^{\frac{5}{2}}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\log|\varepsilon||.

Thus, we obtain

(4.19)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\psi^{(1)}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{5}{2}}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}})|\log|\varepsilon||,\\
&\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial^{2}_{Y}\psi_{s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}}\alpha)|\log|\varepsilon||,\\
&\|\psi^{(1)}_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi_{s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha^{2})|\log|\varepsilon||.\end{split}

From (
4.19
), we infer that

\displaystyle|\psi^{(1)}_{s}(0)|\leq C(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha^{2})|\log|\varepsilon||,\quad|\partial_{Y}\psi^{(1)}_{s}(0)|\leq C\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle OS[\varphi_{Ray}+\psi_{s}^{(1)}]=u^{\prime\prime}\psi_{s}^{(1)}.

Next we define
\tilde{\phi}_{s}\in\mathcal{X}
to be the solution to
OS[\tilde{\phi}_{s}]=-u^{\prime\prime}\psi^{(1)}_{s}
. Then by Theorem
4.1
and (
4.19
),
we can obtain

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\phi}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\leq

\displaystyle C|\log|\varepsilon||(\|\psi^{(1)}_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Y}\psi^{(1)}_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}+\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial^{2}_{Y}\psi^{(1)}_{s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha^{2})|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}.

We finish our construction by defining
\phi_{s}=\varphi_{Ray}+\psi_{s}^{(1)}+\tilde{\phi}_{s}
and applying Proposition
2.6
with
c
replaced by
\hat{c}
.

To construct the neutral stable mode, we need to obtain the asymptotic expansion of
\phi_{s}(0)
up to the order
\alpha^{2}
. Hence, the above bounds of
\psi_{s}^{(1)}(0)
are not sharp enough. In the rest of the proof, we shall provide the exact expansion of
\psi^{(1)}_{s}(0)
up to the order of
\alpha^{2}
for the case
\alpha\gg|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{4}}
.

By (
4.16
), (
4.17
), (
4.18
) and Theorem
3.9
, we have

(4.20)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|\psi_{s}^{(1)}(0)-u^{\prime}(0)^{-1}c_{r}\mathcal{M}_{0,s}|\leq C(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}\ll\alpha^{2},\\
&|\psi_{s}^{(1)}(0)+\mathcal{M}_{0,s}|\leq C(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{5}{2}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}\leq C(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{3}{8}}+|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}\ll\alpha,\end{split}

where
\mathcal{M}_{0,s}
is defined as (
4.25
).

By Proposition
2.6
, Lemma
4.3
and (
4.20
) we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Im}(\varphi_{Ray}(0)+\psi_{s}^{(1)}(0))=-c_{i}+\frac{\alpha c_{r}u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})\pi}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{3}}+o(\alpha^{2}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Im}(\partial_{Y}(\varphi_{Ray}+\psi_{s}^{(1)})(0))=-\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})\pi}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{2}}+o(\alpha).

Now we turn to show that
|\tilde{\phi}_{s}(0)|+\alpha|\partial_{Y}\tilde{\phi}_{s}(0)|\ll\alpha^{2}
. We notice that by (
4.15
), (
4.17
), (
4.18
) and the iteration scheme of
\tilde{\phi}_{s}
introduced in the proof of Theorem
4.1
,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\phi}_{s}-\tilde{\varphi}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{X}}\ll\alpha^{2},\text{ where }Ray_{\hat{c}}[\tilde{\varphi}_{s}]=u^{\prime\prime}\psi_{s}^{(1)},

which implies that

(4.21)

\displaystyle|(\tilde{\phi}_{s}-\tilde{\varphi}_{s})(0)|+\alpha|\partial_{Y}(\tilde{\phi}_{s}-\tilde{\varphi}_{s})(0)|\ll\alpha^{2}

By Proposition
2.1
, we can obtain

(4.22)

\displaystyle|\partial_{Y}\tilde{\varphi}_{s}(0)|\leq C\alpha^{2}|\log|\varepsilon||^{3}\ll\alpha.

To obtain the estimates for
\tilde{\varphi}_{s}(0)
, we decompose
\tilde{\varphi}_{s}=\tilde{\varphi}_{1}+\tilde{\varphi}_{2}
, where

\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}_{1}(Y)=(u-\hat{c})\int_{Y}^{+\infty}(u(Y^{\prime})-\hat{c})^{-2}\int_{Y^{\prime}}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)\psi_{s}^{(1)}(Z)dZdY^{\prime},\quad Ray_{\hat{c}}[\tilde{\varphi}_{2}]=\alpha^{2}(u-\hat{c})\tilde{\varphi}_{1}.

We get by integration by parts that

\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}_{1}(0)=u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)\psi_{s}^{(1)}(Z)dZ+\tilde{\mathcal{R}},

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{R}}=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{c}}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})(u(Y_{0})-\hat{c})}\int_{Y_{0}}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}\psi_{s}^{(1)}dZ+\frac{\hat{c}}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}\int_{0}^{Y_{0}}\frac{u^{\prime\prime}\psi_{s}^{(1)}}{u-\hat{c}}dZ

\displaystyle-\hat{c}\int_{Y_{0}}^{+\infty}(u(Y)-\hat{c})^{-2}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}\psi_{s}^{(1)}dZdY+\frac{\hat{c}}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}\int_{0}^{Y_{0}}\frac{u^{\prime}(Y)-u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}{(u-\hat{c})^{2}}\int_{Y}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}\psi_{s}^{(1)}dZdY.

We notice that by integration by parts,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)\psi_{s}^{(1)}(Z)dZ=-u^{\prime}(0)\psi_{s}^{(1)}(0)-c\partial_{Y}\psi_{s}^{(1)}(0)+\int_{0}^{+\infty}(u-c)\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{s}^{(1)}dY,

which along with (
4.19
) and (
4.20
) implies that

\displaystyle\Big|u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)\psi_{s}^{(1)}(Z)dZ\Big|

\displaystyle\leq C(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|\log|\varepsilon||^{2}+C\|(u-\hat{c})^{2}\partial_{Y}^{2}\psi_{s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-\eta_{0}Z}|u-\hat{c}|^{-1}dZ

\displaystyle\leq C(|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}})|\log|\varepsilon||^{3}\ll\alpha^{2}.

By (
4.19
), we have

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathcal{R}}|\leq C|\hat{c}|\|\psi_{s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+C|\hat{c}|\|\psi_{s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{0}^{Y_{0}}|u-\hat{c}|^{-1}dZ\leq C\alpha^{3}|\log|\varepsilon||^{3}\ll\alpha^{2}.

Therefore, we obtain

(4.23)

\displaystyle|\tilde{\varphi}_{1}(0)|\ll\alpha^{2}.

Using the bounds of
\varphi_{non}^{(0)}
from the proof of Proposition 3.2 in
[
27
]
for the case
m=0
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\varphi}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C|\log|\varepsilon||\|\psi_{3,s}^{(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\alpha^{2}|\log|\varepsilon||^{3}.

From the above bounds and Proposition
2.1
, we infer that

(4.24)

\displaystyle|\tilde{\varphi}_{2}(0)|\leq\|\tilde{\varphi}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\eta_{0}}}\leq C\alpha^{4}|\log|\varepsilon||^{4}\ll\alpha^{2}.

Gathering (
4.23
) and (
4.24
), we conclude

\displaystyle|\tilde{\varphi}_{s}(0)|\ll\alpha^{2},

which along with (
4.21
) and (
4.22
) deduces that

\displaystyle|\tilde{\phi}_{s}(0)|+\alpha|\partial_{Y}\tilde{\phi}_{s}(0)|\ll\alpha^{2}.

The proof is completed.
∎

We define

(4.25)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{0,s}:=\mathcal{M}_{0}[-\varepsilon\chi H_{2}]=\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}(-\varepsilon\chi H_{2})(Z)dZ,

where
\mathcal{H}_{1}
is defined in (
3.12
)

Lemma 4.3
.

Let
\hat{c}:=c+\mathrm{i}|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}
. Suppose
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{3}
with
c_{i}>-|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}/2
and
\alpha\gg|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{4}}
. Then we have

\displaystyle|\mathrm{Re}\left(\mathcal{M}_{0,s}\right)|\leq C\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||\text{ and }\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{M}_{0,s})=\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{2}}\arg(-\hat{c})+\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})\pi}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{2}}+o(\alpha).

Proof.

We can write

(4.26)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{M}_{0,s}=&\underbrace{-\frac{\alpha\varepsilon}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)u^{\prime\prime}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}(u(Z)-\hat{c})^{-1}\chi(Z)dZ}_{\mathcal{M}_{1,s}}\\
&\underbrace{-\varepsilon\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}\chi(Z)H_{2,err}(Z)dZ}_{\mathcal{M}_{2,s}},\end{split}

where

\displaystyle H_{2,err}(Z):=H_{2}(Z)-\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}(Y)}{2u^{\prime}(Y_{c})(u-\hat{c})}.

By Lemma
4.4
, we first have

(4.27)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|\mathcal{M}_{1,s}|\leq C\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||,\quad\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{M}_{1,s})=\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{2}}\arg(-\hat{c})+\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})\pi}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{\frac{4}{3}}}+o(\alpha).\end{split}

We denote that
\tilde{Y}_{0}\geq Y_{c}
such that
\tilde{\eta}_{r}(Y_{0})=c_{r}
, and we write

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{2,s}=\Big(\int_{0}^{Y_{0}}+\int_{Y_{0}}^{+\infty}\Big)\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta^{-1}(\varepsilon\chi(Z)H_{2,err}(Z))dZ

By Lemma
3.4
and (
4.13
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{Y_{0}}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}(\varepsilon\chi(Z)H_{2,err}(Z))dZ\right|\leq C\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||\int_{0}^{Y_{0}}dZ\leq C\alpha^{2}|\log|\varepsilon||\ll\alpha.

On the other hand, by a similar argument for
\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(1)}
as in the proof of Proposition
3.7
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{Y_{0}}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}(\varepsilon\chi(Z)H_{2,err}(Z))dZ\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\log|\varepsilon||\ll\alpha.

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{M}_{2,s}\right|\leq C|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{3}},

which along with (
4.26
) and (
4.27
) implies that

\displaystyle|\mathrm{Re}\left(\mathcal{M}_{0,s}\right)|\leq C\alpha|\log|\varepsilon||\text{ and }\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{M}_{0,s})=\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{2}}\arg(-\hat{c})+\frac{\alpha u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})\pi}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}+o(\alpha).

The proof is completed.
∎

Lemma 4.4
.

Let
\hat{c}:=c+\mathrm{i}|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}
. Suppose
(\alpha,c)\in\mathbb{H}_{3}
with
c_{i}>-|\varepsilon|\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}/2
and
\alpha\gg|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{4}}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Re}\left(\varepsilon\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\frac{(\partial_{Z}^{2}u)\chi(Z)}{(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Z}\eta}dZ\right)\right|\leq C|\log|\varepsilon||,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Im}\left(\varepsilon\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\frac{(\partial_{Z}^{2}u)\chi(Z)}{(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Z}\eta}dZ\right)=-\frac{u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}\arg(-\hat{c})-\frac{u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})\pi}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}+\mathcal{O}(|\varepsilon|^{\frac{1}{24}}+c_{r}^{2}|\varepsilon|^{-\frac{1}{3}}),

where
\chi(Y)\equiv 1
on
[0,\frac{1}{2}]
and
\chi(Y)\equiv 0
on
[1,+\infty)
.

Proof.

We denote
\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)=\mathcal{H}_{1}^{(1)}(Z)+\mathcal{H}_{1}^{(2)}(Z)
with

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1}^{(1)}(Z):=\partial_{Y}A_{1}A_{2}-\partial_{Z}A_{2}A_{1},\quad\mathcal{H}_{1}^{(2)}(Z):=A_{2}(1,Z)\partial_{Z}^{2}A_{1}-A_{1}(1,Z)\partial_{Z}^{2}A_{2}.

Then we can write

\displaystyle\varepsilon\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}(Z)\frac{(\partial_{Z}^{2}u)\chi(Z)}{(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Z}\eta}dZ

\displaystyle\quad=\varepsilon\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}^{(1)}(Z)\frac{(\partial_{Z}^{2}u)\chi(Z)}{(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Z}\eta}d+\varepsilon\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1}^{(2)}(Z)\frac{(\partial_{Z}^{2}u)\chi(Z)}{(u-\hat{c})\partial_{Z}\eta}dZ:=\mathcal{I}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{2}.

By (
3.5
), we obtain that for any
Z\geq 0
,
\mathcal{H}_{1}^{(1)}(Z)\partial_{Y}\eta(Z)^{-1}\equiv\varepsilon^{-1}
,
which implies that

(4.28)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}=\int_{0}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)(u(Z)-\hat{c})^{-1}\chi(Z)dZ.

On the other hand, we know that

(4.29)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{L}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)(u(Z)-\hat{c})^{-1}\chi(Z)dZ\Big|+\hat{c}^{-1}_{i}\Big|\mathrm{Im}\Big(\int_{L}^{+\infty}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)(u(Z)-\hat{c})^{-1}\chi(Z)dZ\Big)\Big|\leq C.

Hence, we only need to show the estimates for
\int_{0}^{L}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)(u(Z)-\hat{c})^{-1}\chi(Z)dZ
. Notice that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{L}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)(u(Z)-\hat{c})^{-1}\chi(Z)dZ=-\frac{u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}\log(-\hat{c})+\mathcal{I}_{Err,1},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{Err,1}=

\displaystyle\frac{u^{\prime\prime}(L)}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}\log(u(L)-\hat{c})+\frac{u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})-u^{\prime\prime}(0)}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}\log(-\hat{c})+u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-1}\int_{0}^{L}\frac{(u^{\prime}(Y_{c})-u^{\prime}(Z))u^{\prime\prime}\chi}{u(Z)-\hat{c}}dZ

\displaystyle-u^{\prime}(Y_{c})^{-1}\int_{0}^{L}\log(u(Z)-\hat{c})\partial_{Z}[u^{\prime}(Z)\chi]dZ.

We can easily obtain

(4.30)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\mathrm{Re}\left(\mathcal{I}_{Err,1}\right)\right|\leq C|\log|\varepsilon||,\quad\left|\mathrm{Im}\left(\mathcal{I}_{Err,1}\right)\right|\leq C|c|.\end{split}

Therefore, by (
4.28
), (
4.29
) and (
4.30
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{I}_{1})\right|\leq C|\log|\varepsilon||,\quad\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{I}_{1})=-\frac{u^{\prime\prime}(Y_{c})}{u^{\prime}(Y_{c})}\arg(-\hat{c})+\mathcal{O}(|c|).

Now we turn to show the estimates for
\mathcal{I}_{2}
. By the definition of
A_{1}
and
A_{2}
, we notice that

(4.31)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathcal{I}_{2}=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1,1}^{(2)}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)\partial_{Y}\eta(Z)^{-1}\chi(Z)dZ}_{\mathcal{I}_{2,1}}+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1,err1}(Z)u^{\prime\prime}(Z)\partial_{Y}\eta(Z)^{-1}\chi(Z)dZ}_{\mathcal{I}_{2,2}}\end{split}

with

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1,1}^{(2)}=A_{2}(1,Z)A_{1}(Z)-A_{1}(1,Z)A_{2}(Z),

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1,err1}(Z)=A_{2}(1,Z)(u(Z)-\hat{c})^{-1}(Err_{1}(Z)A_{1}(Z)+Err_{2}(Z)\partial_{Y}A_{1}(Z))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-A_{1}(1,Z)(u(Z)-\hat{c})^{-1}(Err_{1}(Z)A_{2}(Z)+Err_{2}(Z)\partial_{Y}A_{2}(Z)).

Notice that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2,1}=\int_{0}^{2Y_{c}}\mathcal{H}_{1,1}^{(2)}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)\partial_{Z}\eta(Z)^{-1}dZ+\underbrace{\int_{2Y_{c}}^{+\infty}\mathcal{H}_{1,1}^{(2)}u^{\prime\prime}(Z)\partial_{Y}\eta(Z)^{-1}\chi(Z)dZ}_{\mathcal{R}_{2,1}}.

Moreover, we have
